# T2.5 filesystem 安全操作实现计划

> **面向 AI 代理的工作者：** 必需子技能：使用
> `superpowers:subagent-driven-development`（推荐）或
> `superpowers:executing-plans` 逐任务实现此计划。步骤使用复选框（`- [ ]`）
> 语法跟踪进度；每个任务提交前必须先做独立规格审查，再做独立代码质量审查。

**目标：** 实现 Windows 上失败关闭的 `internal/filesystem`：以固定祖先和 leaf
句柄完成路径授权、Runtime 日志、state、download staging、受控递归删除及原子
rename，确保检查到副作用之间没有按路径重新打开的替换窗口。

**架构：** `path_windows.go` 统一规范路径、查询物理身份并建立不授予
`FILE_SHARE_DELETE` 的祖先 pin；三类专用能力分别把 Runtime 日志 token、state
snapshot 和 download session 绑定到 owner、volume serial、128-bit file ID 与同一
打开句柄。StateFiles 以每 kind 持久普通 guard `G` 跨实例线性化 namespace 决策，
以 sealed intent、全程 no-replace rename 和经探测证明的 POSIX namespace unlink
实现写入、恢复和条件删除。通用 `Operator` 只接受封闭的 `DeleteKind` /
`RenameKind`，重新用 `config.Layout` 绑定精确身份，递归删除和 rename 均以
handle-relative Win32 API 执行；副作用成功后先冻结 `MutationApplied` /
`Published` / `Removed`，再报告关闭错误。State 写入以七阶段
`StateWriteError` 和 `MutationApplied` / `RecoveryRequired` 双事实报告主错误与
独立 cleanup 错误，不要求消费方解析诊断字符串。

**技术栈：** Go 1.26，标准库 `context`、`crypto/sha256`、`errors`、`io`、
`path/filepath`、`sync`、`time`、`testing`；固定依赖
`golang.org/x/sys/windows v0.42.0`；Windows 10/11、PowerShell 7、Win32
`CreateFileW` / `GetFileInformationByHandleEx` /
`SetFileInformationByHandle` / `DuplicateHandle` / `FlushFileBuffers`。

**设计依据：** [T2.5 filesystem 安全操作设计](./设计-T2.5-filesystem安全操作.md)、
[T2.3 state 持久化设计](./设计-T2.3-state持久化.md)、
[架构设计“文件与删除安全边界”](./架构设计.md#文件与删除安全边界)、
[任务拆分 T2.5](./任务拆分.md)。任务 0 在执行时动态记录任务拆分、T2.3/T2.5 设计和
本计划的 SHA-256；计划不固化会随文档修订过期的提交祖先或设计 hash。

---

## 文件结构

- 保留 `internal/filesystem/doc.go`
  - 现有中文包注释已经准确，不修改。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/path_windows.go`
  - 定义公共错误类型、`CanonicalPath`、路径规范化、volume / file ID / link count /
    case-sensitive 查询、固定祖先句柄和 handle-relative Win32 基础操作。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/path_windows_test.go`
  - 白盒测试规范路径、错误链、句柄连续性、UNC / remote /
    per-directory case-sensitive 拒绝和底层故障注入。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/runtime_logs.go`
  - 定义 Runtime 日志能力、writer、不可伪造文件 token、`RemoveResult` 和公共方法。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/runtime_logs_windows.go`
  - 实现日志目录创建与固定、append writer、直接文件列举和 handle disposition 删除。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/runtime_logs_windows_test.go`
  - 测试祖先 pin、硬链接、token 身份、父能力 close 与 writer 独立生命周期。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/state_files.go`
  - 定义 `StateFileKind`、snapshot、七阶段写入错误、写入/删除双事实结果和公共方法。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/state_files_windows.go`
  - 实现持久 per-kind guard、sealed intent、恢复矩阵、no-replace 发布、POSIX
    namespace unlink、有界读取和本实例 Close 线性化。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/state_files_windows_test.go`
  - 测试 exact native gate、sealed/no-replace、完整恢复矩阵、POSIX capability、
    双事实结果、size limit、snapshot 身份、七阶段故障及 barrier 并发。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/download_files.go`
  - 定义 download 能力、session、publish / abort 结果和不可逆状态机。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/download_files_windows.go`
  - 实现 `.part` create-exclusive、同句柄写入、no-replace 发布和失败清理。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/download_files_windows_test.go`
  - 测试 stale part、同名竞争、发布 / abort 状态、file ID 连续性和关闭故障。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/delete.go`
  - 先定义删除枚举、请求、结果、审计接口与审计错误；Task 9 再同时引入
    `Operator`、`Option`、私有 `options` 和构造器。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/delete_windows.go`
  - 实现 Layout 身份授权、逐项递归删除、双阶段审计和独立收口 context。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/delete_test.go`
  - 测试封闭枚举、字段校验、允许列表、保护名单和审计状态机。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/delete_windows_test.go`
  - 测试固定句柄递归、Reparse Point、部分删除、取消和删除故障注入。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/rename.go`
  - 先定义 rename 枚举、请求、结果和等待器；Task 9 再与 `options` 同时引入有限
    重试 Option。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/rename_windows.go`
  - 实现精确源目标授权、固定源与目标父句柄、no-replace rename 和错误映射。
- 创建 `internal/filesystem/rename_windows_test.go`
  - 测试三种 rename、延迟序列、context、占用、竞争目标和 post-rename close 故障。
- 修改 `go.mod`
  - 首个实现任务固定 `golang.org/x/sys v0.42.0`；后续任务只验证，不重复引入或降级。
- 创建 `go.sum`
  - 记录固定依赖校验和。
- 修改 `doc/任务拆分.md`
  - 全部门禁与审查通过后，单独回写 T2.5 完成日期和代码提交短哈希。

首版只提供 `_windows.go` 实现，不添加非 Windows 伪实现。`internal/filesystem`
只能依赖 `internal/config`、`internal/protocol` 和 `golang.org/x/sys/windows`；
不得依赖 `cli`、`gitrepo`、`logging`、`state` 或 `uv`。

## 全局实现约束

1. 所有路径只能来自传入的 `config.Layout` getter；`internal/filesystem` 不硬编码
   `repo`、`runtime-state`、`logs/runtime`、缓存或 state 文件名。
2. 规范化只产生值对象。任何写、删或 rename 必须从 app-root 开始逐级持有不授予
   `FILE_SHARE_DELETE` 的祖先 handle，并在同一个已核验 leaf handle 上完成
   disposition / rename；禁止回退到 `os.RemoveAll`、`os.Remove`、`os.Rename` 或
   路径版 `MoveFileExW`。
3. 每个已存在组件以
   `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT | FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS` 打开。任何
   Reparse Point、无法查询 file ID / volume / attributes / case sensitivity 的组件都
   失败关闭。
4. 危险能力只支持本地 volume。UNC 可以由 `Canonicalize` 解析用于诊断，但
   `Operator`、`RuntimeLogFiles`、`StateFiles`、`DownloadFiles` 拒绝 UNC、
   `DRIVE_REMOTE` 和任一祖先启用 `FILE_CS_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE_DIR` 的布局。
5. 所有接收 `ctx` 的 API 在任何规范化或 I/O 前拒绝 nil；逐级打开、遍历、gate 等待和
   每个新副作用前检查取消。nil 必须包装 `ErrInvalidArgument`，调用前已经取消必须满足
   `errors.Is(err, context.Canceled)`，且两者 canonicalize / gate / probe / Win32 /
   Auditor 调用计数均为 0。带 ctx 的入口及其 helper 只调用
   `canonicalizeContextWith(ctx, ...)`；只有不接收 ctx 的公共 `Canonicalize` 才以
   `context.Background()` 调用该 helper。提交点之后观察到的取消不能覆盖已经冻结的
   applied / recovery / published / removed 结果。生产和测试禁止 `time.Sleep`。
6. `RuntimeLogFiles` 用 mutex 线性化 open/list/remove/close；writer 独立复制三层
   祖先 pin。`StateFiles` 的 RWMutex 协调本实例 closed/root-handle/probe map 生命周期：
   Read 从 closed 检查到 payload leaf 关闭全程持 RLock，WriteAtomic 与
   RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged 从 closed 检查到全部协议 handles/G 关闭全程持 Lock，
   Close 同样持 Lock、先拒绝新操作再关闭 pins。跨实例 namespace 决策全部由持久
   per-kind `G` 完成：Read 取得 shared G，Write/W0/rollback/finalize/conditional
   remove 取得 exclusive G。进程内写锁保护 probe map 和 Close，但不得替代跨实例 G，
   intent 双 probe 也不得替代 G。`DownloadFiles` 构造器只同步验证 Layout 和当前已存在祖先，
   返回前关闭全部校验 handle；对象只保存不可变 Layout 与测试依赖。每次 `Begin` 都
   重新逐级创建、打开并固定 app-root/runtime/cache/downloads 四层祖先，把四层 pin 和
   part leaf 一并交给新 session；不得永久持有 base pin、不得增加公共 `Close` 或
   finalizer。
7. `WriteAtomicResult`、`StateWriteError` 与 `StateRemoveResult` 的
   `MutationApplied/RecoveryRequired`，`RenameResult.MutationApplied`、Runtime 日志
   `RemoveResult.MutationApplied`、`PublishResult.Published` 和 `AbortResult.Removed`
   必须在 Win32 副作用成功后、关闭 handle 前冻结。关闭失败不能抹掉已生效事实，也不能
   自动回滚、重试或再次 Abort。`WriteAtomic` 一旦开始 I/O，recover / create / write /
   sync / rename / finalize / close 失败必须返回冻结阶段的 `StateWriteError`；主错误
   只进 `Cause`，rollback / disposition / close 等次因只进 `CleanupError`，二者由
   `Unwrap() []error` 同时保链。条件删除只有主因之后又发生 cleanup/close 错误时才用
   `StateRemoveError` 分离两链；clean mismatch 只返回 `ErrIdentityChanged`。
8. 删除 `started` 审计失败时零副作用；started 成功后必须用
   `context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(operationCtx), 5*time.Second)`
   尝试 finished。删除错误、取消错误和 finished 审计错误使用 `errors.Join` 保留
   每条错误链。
9. 任一 multi-handle close 都尝试关闭全部尚未关闭的 handles，按逆序收集并
   `errors.Join`；单个 close 失败不得短路剩余 close。已经成功关闭的 handle 必须立即
   标记 invalid，失败的 handle 保持原值供同一次收口事实报告，且错误返回不得覆盖此前
   冻结的 mutation/recovery/publish/remove 事实。
10. 测试故障注入只通过同包私有 constructor / function-field dependency 实现，不增加
   公共 Option。每个 public constructor 创建自己的不可变 production `pathAPI`，
   禁止包级可变替身；测试只调用下节冻结的 `...With` 私有入口。测试替身必须响应 ctx。
11. 新增注释全部为中文并以被注释标识符开头；error 字符串使用英文、小写开头、无句号。
    包不写 stdout，不发 protocol 事件。

## 冻结私有依赖与 Windows 打开矩阵

为避免故障注入变成包级竞态，以下私有签名也在计划内冻结。生产 wrapper 每次构造
局部、不可变的 `pathAPI`；测试把 function fields 放入值参数，不修改包级变量。

```go
type objectIdentity struct {
	volumeSerial  uint64
	fileID        [16]byte
	attributes    uint32
	numberOfLinks uint32
	size          int64
}

type directoryEntry struct {
	name       string
	attributes uint32
}

type openSpec struct {
	access    uint32
	share     uint32
	creation  uint32
	options   uint32
	directory bool
}

type ntCreateSpec struct {
	desiredAccess     uint32
	shareAccess       uint32
	createDisposition uint32
	createOptions     uint32
}

type stateDispositionSpec struct {
	informationClass uint32
	flags            uint32
}

type pathAPI struct {
	makeDirectory       func(path string) error
	attributes          func(path string) (uint32, error)
	openPath            func(path string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error)
	openRelative        func(parent windows.Handle, name string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error)
	ntCreateRelative    func(parent windows.Handle, name string, spec ntCreateSpec) (windows.Handle, error)
	finalPath           func(handle windows.Handle) (string, error)
	identity            func(handle windows.Handle) (objectIdentity, error)
	caseSensitive       func(handle windows.Handle) (bool, error)
	driveType           func(root string) (uint32, error)
	duplicateHandle     func(handle windows.Handle) (windows.Handle, error)
	listDirectory       func(handle windows.Handle) ([]directoryEntry, error)
	readFile            func(handle windows.Handle, buffer []byte) (int, error)
	writeFile           func(handle windows.Handle, buffer []byte) (int, error)
	flushFile           func(handle windows.Handle) error
	setDisposition      func(handle windows.Handle) error
	setStateDisposition func(
		handle windows.Handle,
		spec stateDispositionSpec,
	) error
	rename func(
		source windows.Handle,
		targetParent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		replace bool,
	) error
	renameState func(
		source windows.Handle,
		targetParent windows.Handle,
		name string,
		flags uint32,
	) error
	closeHandle func(handle windows.Handle) error
}

func newProductionPathAPI() pathAPI
func (a pathAPI) valid() bool

type pinnedObject struct {
	path     CanonicalPath
	handle   windows.Handle
	identity objectIdentity
}

type pinnedChain struct {
	api     pathAPI
	objects []pinnedObject
}

func openPinnedChain(
	ctx context.Context,
	rootParent CanonicalPath,
	target CanonicalPath,
	leafSpec openSpec,
) (*pinnedChain, error)

func openPinnedChainWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	rootParent CanonicalPath,
	target CanonicalPath,
	leafSpec openSpec,
	api pathAPI,
) (*pinnedChain, error)

func validateDangerousRoot(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	targets []string,
) ([]*pinnedChain, error)

func validateDangerousRootWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	targets []string,
	api pathAPI,
) ([]*pinnedChain, error)

func openRelativeCheckedWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	parent pinnedObject,
	name string,
	spec openSpec,
	api pathAPI,
) (pinnedObject, error)

func validateParentIdentityWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	parent pinnedObject,
	child pinnedObject,
	api pathAPI,
) error

func deleteByHandleWith(handle windows.Handle, api pathAPI) error
func renameByHandleWith(
	source windows.Handle,
	targetParent windows.Handle,
	name string,
	replace bool,
	api pathAPI,
) error
func (c *pinnedChain) close() error
```

`validateParentIdentityWith` 从 child 的最终 handle path 取得物理父路径，在 fixed parent
仍持有且不授予 share-delete 时以 open-reparse-point 打开物理父目录，比较 volume
serial 和 128-bit file ID 后立即关闭验证 handle；不接受字符串相等替代 file ID。
`pinnedChain.close` 必须使用 `c.api.closeHandle` 逆序关闭，确保注入的 close 故障与打开
该 chain 的同一个 API 值配对。

owner marker 必须是非零大小的私有类型，避免不同零大小对象的指针被 Go 实现合并：

```go
type runtimeLogOwner struct {
	marker byte
}

type stateOwner struct {
	marker byte
}
```

四类能力和派生对象固定按以下方式保存、复制同一个 `pathAPI` 值；任何方法都不得临时改用
`newProductionPathAPI()`，否则测试无法覆盖 write / close / disposition / rename 故障：

```go
type RuntimeLogFiles struct {
	mu sync.Mutex // 保护 closed、closeErr、pins 以及 open/list/remove/close 的线性化。

	layout   *config.Layout
	api      pathAPI
	owner    *runtimeLogOwner
	pins     [3]pinnedObject
	closed   bool
	closeErr error
}

type RuntimeLogWriter struct {
	mu sync.Mutex // 保护 file、pins、closed 和 closeErr。

	api      pathAPI
	path     string
	file     pinnedObject
	pins     [3]pinnedObject
	closed   bool
	closeErr error
}

type RuntimeLogFile struct {
	owner        *runtimeLogOwner
	path         string
	name         string
	volumeSerial uint64
	fileID       [16]byte
}

type StateFiles struct {
	mu sync.RWMutex // 保护 closed、closeErr、pins、probe 状态及本实例操作与 Close 的线性化。

	layout      *config.Layout
	api         pathAPI
	owner       *stateOwner
	pins        [2]pinnedObject
	waitGate    func(context.Context, time.Duration) error
	fillNonce   func([]byte) error
	probePassed map[StateFileKind]bool
	closed      bool
	closeErr    error
}

type StateFileSnapshot struct {
	owner        *stateOwner
	kind         StateFileKind
	volumeSerial uint64
	fileID       [16]byte
	size         int64
	digest       [sha256.Size]byte
	payload      []byte
}

type DownloadFiles struct {
	layout *config.Layout
	api    pathAPI
}

type DownloadSession struct {
	mu sync.Mutex // 保护 part、pins、state、结果和 closeErr。

	api       pathAPI
	path      string
	partPath  string
	part      pinnedObject
	pins      [4]pinnedObject
	state     downloadState
	published PublishResult
	abort     AbortResult
	closeErr  error
}
```

`NewRuntimeLogFiles`、`NewStateFiles`、`NewDownloadFiles` 和 `New` 仅在公共入口创建一次
`newProductionPathAPI()`；private constructor 校验 `api.valid()` 后把该值存入返回对象。
`OpenAppend` 把 `f.api` 复制到 writer，`Begin` 把 `f.api` 复制到 session，
`openPinnedChainWith` 把实参复制到 chain，`New` 把 dependencies.api 存入 Operator；
派生方法和清理路径只能继续使用已保存值。测试从完整 production API 值复制一份后仅替换
目标字段，不允许 nil 未使用字段掩盖依赖遗漏。

Download session 没有公共 Close。故障注入测试需要强制释放仍打开 handle 时，在
`download_files_windows_test.go`（同包测试）精确定义：

```go
func (s *DownloadSession) closeForTest() error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	closeErrors := make([]error, 0, len(s.pins)+1)
	closeOne := func(handle *windows.Handle) {
		if *handle == 0 || *handle == windows.InvalidHandle {
			return
		}
		if err := s.api.closeHandle(*handle); err != nil {
			closeErrors = append(closeErrors, err)
			return
		}
		*handle = windows.InvalidHandle
	}
	closeOne(&s.part.handle)
	for i := len(s.pins) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		closeOne(&s.pins[i].handle)
	}
	return errors.Join(closeErrors...)
}
```

该 helper 持有 `s.mu`，只用 `s.api.closeHandle` 关闭仍打开的 part 与四层 pins，
不 disposition、不 rename、不改变公开状态机；只允许由 `t.Cleanup` 调用。生产文件、
消费包和正常绿灯路径不得引用它。`DownloadFiles` 本身没有 handle，因此不得定义
`(*DownloadFiles).closeForTest`。

各能力 private constructor 固定为：

```go
type runtimeLogDependencies struct {
	api pathAPI
}

func newRuntimeLogFilesWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	dependencies runtimeLogDependencies,
) (*RuntimeLogFiles, error)

type stateFileDependencies struct {
	api       pathAPI
	waitGate  func(context.Context, time.Duration) error
	fillNonce func([]byte) error
}

func newStateFilesWith(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	dependencies stateFileDependencies,
) (*StateFiles, error)

type downloadFileDependencies struct {
	api pathAPI
}

func newDownloadFilesWith(
	layout *config.Layout,
	dependencies downloadFileDependencies,
) (*DownloadFiles, error)

type operatorDependencies struct {
	api             pathAPI
	finishedContext func(context.Context) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc)
}

func newWithDependencies(
	ctx context.Context,
	layout *config.Layout,
	auditor Auditor,
	opts options,
	dependencies operatorDependencies,
) (*Operator, error)
```

`Operator` 在 Task 9 定义时必须包含 `layout *config.Layout`、`auditor Auditor`、
`api pathAPI`、`wait WaitFunc`、防御性复制的 `delays []time.Duration` 和
`finishedContext`；构造验证使用的临时 chains 在返回前全部关闭，操作时再用 `o.api`
重新固定路径。

除 State guard `G` 外，所有 `CreateFileW` / relative open 都必须从下表选择完整
`openSpec`，并在测试中逐字段断言 access、share、creation、options 和 directory；
任何能力不得自行拼另一组权限。`openRelativeWindows` 把这些 Win32-style spec 转成
`NtCreateFile` 时，只要 access 含 `SYNCHRONIZE`，就必须加入
`FILE_SYNCHRONOUS_IO_NONALERT`；若不含则拒绝该旧入口，避免它污染单列的异步 G 参数。

| 能力 | access | share | creation | options | directory |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Canonicalize 已存在前缀 | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | 按已存在前缀实际类型 |
| 目录 pin / list root / rename target parent | `FILE_LIST_DIRECTORY \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `true` |
| parent-identity 验证 handle | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `true` |
| Runtime log append leaf | `FILE_APPEND_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_ALWAYS` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Runtime log List leaf | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Runtime log Remove leaf | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State Read/old/new 权威 payload leaf | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State `T/J` create-exclusive leaf | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_WRITE_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `CREATE_NEW` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State `D/O/B` strong identity anchor | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State sealed `I` identity anchor | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State conditional Remove identity anchor | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| State POSIX probe identity anchor `A` | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_WRITE_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `CREATE_NEW` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT \| FILE_FLAG_DELETE_ON_CLOSE` | `false` |
| State POSIX unlink `U` | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Download final inspection | `FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE \| FILE_SHARE_DELETE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Download stale part removal | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| Download new part | `FILE_WRITE_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| DELETE \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ` | `CREATE_NEW` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| 递归删除 file | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `false` |
| 递归删除 directory | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| FILE_LIST_DIRECTORY \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `true` |
| Rename source directory | `DELETE \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES \| SYNCHRONIZE` | `FILE_SHARE_READ \| FILE_SHARE_WRITE` | `OPEN_EXISTING` | `FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS \| FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT` | `true` |

Canonicalize 的前缀 open 是只读快照，允许 share-delete；任何危险能力随后仍须重新从
app-root 固定祖先，不能复用已经关闭的规范化 handle。parent-identity 行只用于打开 child
最终物理路径的父目录，并与仍固定的 parent 比较 file ID；不得复用 Canonicalize 行。
State strong handle 保持到对应 rename/unlink/恢复与身份复验完成，并要求 ordinary、
非 Reparse、`NumberOfLinks == 1` 和 parent identity 一致；不存在只能由持有 G 的
namespace snapshot 授权，不能由 raw not-found 自动推导。
Runtime log append leaf 的 share 必须精确等于
`windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE`，明确不含
`windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE`；这既允许不同 `RuntimeLogFiles` 实例同时 append，又使
任一活动 writer 都能阻止 leaf 被删除、重命名或替换。

State guard `G` 不经过 `openSpec`，只经过 `pathAPI.ntCreateRelative`。生产和测试逐次
精确断言：

| gate 模式 | DesiredAccess | ShareAccess | CreateDisposition | CreateOptions |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| reader create-exclusive | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES` | `FILE_SHARE_READ` | `FILE_CREATE` | `FILE_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT \| FILE_NON_DIRECTORY_FILE` |
| reader collision 后 open | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES` | `FILE_SHARE_READ` | `FILE_OPEN` | `FILE_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT \| FILE_NON_DIRECTORY_FILE` |
| exclusive create-exclusive | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES` | `0` | `FILE_CREATE` | `FILE_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT \| FILE_NON_DIRECTORY_FILE` |
| exclusive collision 后 open | `FILE_READ_DATA \| FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES` | `0` | `FILE_OPEN` | `FILE_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT \| FILE_NON_DIRECTORY_FILE` |

DesiredAccess 明确不含 `SYNCHRONIZE`/write/delete，CreateOptions 明确不含
`FILE_SYNCHRONOUS_IO_ALERT` / `FILE_SYNCHRONOUS_IO_NONALERT`。attributes-only、
DesiredAccess `0`、额外 `SYNCHRONIZE`、任一 synchronous option、`FILE_OPEN_IF` /
overwrite/supersede、share-write/share-delete、reader share `0` 或 exclusive
share-read 均为 `TestStateFiles_GuardSerializesNamespaceDecisions` 的独立 invalid
反例。

每个 leaf 打开成功后必须调用带 ctx 的
`validateParentIdentityWith(ctx, parent, child, api)`，失败时通过同一个
`api.closeHandle` 关闭 leaf 且零副作用。Task 1～11 的权限测试合起来必须实际触发上表
每一行，并逐字段比较五列，不能只断言 access 或 share。

带 context 的 14 个公共 API 与零 I/O 测试固定映射如下；每个测试均表驱动覆盖 nil 和
调用前已取消两行，不能只覆盖其中一种：

| 公共 API | 固定测试 |
| --- | --- |
| `New` | `TestNew_ContextRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `NewRuntimeLogFiles` / `OpenAppend` | `TestRuntimeLogFiles_ContextRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `List` / `Remove` | `TestRuntimeLogFiles_ListAndRemoveContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `NewStateFiles` / `Read` | `TestStateFiles_ReadContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `WriteAtomic` / `RemoveTransactionIfUnchanged` | `TestStateFiles_WriteAndRemoveContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `Begin` | `TestDownloadFiles_BeginContextRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `PublishNoReplace` / `Abort` | `TestDownloadSession_PublishAndAbortContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `RemoveTree` | `TestRemoveTree_ContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |
| `AtomicRename` | `TestAtomicRename_ContextsRejectedBeforeIO` |

## 公共 PowerShell 7 前置与 helper

每个任务开始时在
`D:\Github\AUTO-MAS-Runtime\.worktrees\codex-runtime-implementation`
使用 `pwsh` 执行：

```powershell
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$go = (Get-Command go -ErrorAction Stop).Source
$gofmt = (Get-Command gofmt -ErrorAction Stop).Source
$rg = (Get-Command rg -ErrorAction Stop).Source
$env:GOCACHE = Join-Path $env:TEMP "auto-mas-runtime-t25"

function Invoke-Checked {
    param(
        [scriptblock]$Command,
        [string]$Name
    )

    $output = @(& $Command 2>&1)
    $exitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    $output
    if ($exitCode -ne 0) {
        throw "$Name failed with exit code $exitCode"
    }
}

function Invoke-ExpectedFailure {
    param(
        [string]$Pattern,
        [string]$ExpectedFailure
    )

    $output = @(& $go test ./internal/filesystem -run $Pattern -count=1 -v 2>&1)
    $exitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    $output
    $text = $output -join [Environment]::NewLine
    if ($exitCode -eq 0) {
        throw "red test unexpectedly passed"
    }
    if ($text -match "\[no tests to run\]" -or
        $text -notmatch "(build failed|--- FAIL:)") {
        throw "red test did not execute and fail"
    }
    if ($text -notmatch $ExpectedFailure) {
        throw "red test failed for an unexpected reason"
    }
}

function Invoke-PassingTest {
    param(
        [string]$Pattern,
        [int]$Count = 1
    )

    $output = @(& $go test ./internal/filesystem -run $Pattern -count=$Count -v 2>&1)
    $exitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
    $output
    $text = $output -join [Environment]::NewLine
    if ($exitCode -ne 0) {
        throw "targeted test failed with exit code $exitCode"
    }
    if ($text -match "\[no tests to run\]" -or $text -notmatch "--- PASS:") {
        throw "targeted test did not produce a passing result"
    }
}

function Assert-XSysPin {
    param([string]$Task)

    $xsysVersion = (& $go list -m -f '{{.Version}}' "golang.org/x/sys").Trim()
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task x/sys version lookup failed" }
    if ($xsysVersion -ne "v0.42.0") {
        throw "$Task x/sys version = $xsysVersion, want v0.42.0"
    }

    $requirements = @(
        Select-String `
            -LiteralPath "go.mod" `
            -Pattern '^\s*golang\.org/x/sys\s+v0\.42\.0(?:\s+// indirect)?\s*$'
    )
    if ($requirements.Count -ne 1) {
        throw "$Task go.mod must contain exactly one x/sys v0.42.0 requirement"
    }
    $wrongVersions = @(
        Select-String `
            -LiteralPath "go.mod" `
            -Pattern 'golang\.org/x/sys\s+v(?!0\.42\.0(?:\s|$))\S+'
    )
    if ($wrongVersions.Count -ne 0) {
        $wrongVersions
        throw "$Task found a conflicting x/sys version"
    }
}

function Invoke-StandardGate {
    param([string]$Task)

    Assert-XSysPin -Task $Task

    $unformatted = @(& $gofmt -l .)
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task gofmt check failed" }
    if ($unformatted.Count -ne 0) {
        $unformatted
        throw "$Task gofmt found unformatted files"
    }

    & $go vet ./...
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task go vet failed" }
    & $go build -buildvcs=false ./...
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task go build failed" }
    & $go test ./... -count=1
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task go test failed" }
    & git diff --check
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task git diff check failed" }
}

function Submit-Task {
    param(
        [string]$Task,
        [string]$Message,
        [string[]]$Files
    )

    Invoke-StandardGate -Task $Task

    $before = @(
        & git -c core.quotePath=false diff --cached --name-only
    )
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task pre-stage check failed" }
    if ($before.Count -ne 0) {
        $before
        throw "$Task found pre-existing staged files"
    }

    $stagedByInvocation = @()
    try {
        & git add -- $Files
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task staging failed" }

        $staged = @(
            & git -c core.quotePath=false diff --cached --name-only
        )
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task staged list failed" }
        $stagedByInvocation = @(
            $staged | Where-Object { $_ -notin $before }
        )
        $delta = @(
            Compare-Object `
                ($stagedByInvocation | Sort-Object) `
                ($Files | Sort-Object)
        )
        if ($delta.Count -ne 0) {
            $delta
            throw "$Task staged unexpected files"
        }

        & git diff --cached --check
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task staged diff check failed" }
        & git commit -m $Message
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Task commit failed" }
    } catch {
        if ($stagedByInvocation.Count -eq 0) {
            $current = @(
                & git -c core.quotePath=false diff --cached --name-only
            )
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
                $stagedByInvocation = @(
                    $current |
                        Where-Object {
                            $_ -notin $before -and $_ -in $Files
                        }
                )
            }
        }
        if ($stagedByInvocation.Count -ne 0) {
            & git restore --staged -- $stagedByInvocation
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
                throw "$Task failed and could not restore its staged files"
            }
        }
        throw
    }
}
```

所有原生命令都必须立即读取并检查 `$LASTEXITCODE`。定向测试必须同时证明退出码为 0、
输出不含 `[no tests to run]` 且出现 `--- PASS:`。红灯必须验证预期 API 缺失或指定
断言失败，不能接受工具链、权限或测试正则错误。`Submit-Task` 在任何 staging / commit
之前强制执行完整 gofmt / vet / build / test / diff-check 标准门，因此 Task 1～13 的
每个非空提交都必须以当次全绿证据为前提。两个 staged-file 快照都关闭
`core.quotePath`，中文路径按原样比较；helper 拒绝已有暂存内容，失败时只
`git restore --staged` 本次调用新增的文件，不修改用户原有暂存、工作树内容或提交历史。

## 任务 0：实现前依赖、动态 planning hash、计划与只读基线硬门

**文件：**

- 不修改任何文件。

本任务只验证前置条件并在当前 PowerShell 会话记录动态 baseline。任一检查失败都立即
停止，不删除、移动、暂存或藏匿现场，不用 `git clean`、`git reset`、`git stash`、
`git checkout`、`git restore` 或写操作代替用户裁定。

- [ ] **步骤 1：确认 worktree、分支与 T2.1**

```powershell
$expectedRoot = (
    Resolve-Path -LiteralPath `
        "D:\Github\AUTO-MAS-Runtime\.worktrees\codex-runtime-implementation" `
        -ErrorAction Stop
).Path
$actualRootText = (& git rev-parse --show-toplevel).Trim()
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 worktree lookup failed" }
$actualRoot = (
    Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $actualRootText -ErrorAction Stop
).Path
if (-not [StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase.Equals(
    $actualRoot,
    $expectedRoot
)) {
    throw "T2.5 unexpected worktree: $actualRoot"
}

$branch = (& git branch --show-current).Trim()
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 branch lookup failed" }
if ($branch -ne "codex/runtime-implementation") {
    throw "T2.5 unexpected branch: $branch"
}

$t21CompletionCommit = "ab927a1"
& git merge-base --is-ancestor $t21CompletionCommit HEAD
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    throw "T2.1 completion commit is not an ancestor of HEAD"
}
$t21Progress = @(
    & $rg -n --fixed-strings -- `
        '- [x] **T2.1 config 与目录布局**（M）' `
        "doc/任务拆分.md"
)
$t21ProgressExit = $LASTEXITCODE
$t21Progress
if ($t21ProgressExit -gt 1) { throw "T2.1 progress lookup failed" }
if ($t21ProgressExit -eq 1 -or $t21Progress.Count -ne 1) {
    throw "T2.1 must satisfy DoD and be recorded complete before T2.5"
}

& $go test ./internal/config -count=100
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.1 config tests failed" }
```

预期：Git 返回的 `/` 路径与固定 worktree 的 `\` 路径都先经 `Resolve-Path`
规范为绝对路径，再按 `OrdinalIgnoreCase` 精确匹配；分支精确匹配，T2.1 的实现提交和
进度记录均已进入当前历史，`internal/config` 重复测试通过。T2.1 进度尚未回写时，先
执行已经批准的 T2.1 收口计划并由用户处理提交；不得在 T2.5 实现提交中顺带修改。

- [ ] **步骤 2：确认本计划获批、已提交，并记录四个 planning hash**

```powershell
$planPath = "doc/计划-T2.5-filesystem安全操作.md"
$trackedPlan = @(& git ls-files --error-unmatch -- $planPath)
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or $trackedPlan.Count -ne 1) {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan must be tracked and committed"
}

& git diff --quiet -- $planPath
$planWorktreeExit = $LASTEXITCODE
if ($planWorktreeExit -gt 1) { throw "T2.5 plan worktree check failed" }
if ($planWorktreeExit -eq 1) {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan has uncommitted worktree changes"
}

& git diff --cached --quiet -- $planPath
$planIndexExit = $LASTEXITCODE
if ($planIndexExit -gt 1) { throw "T2.5 plan index check failed" }
if ($planIndexExit -eq 1) {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan has uncommitted staged changes"
}

$planCommit = (& git log -1 --format=%H -- $planPath).Trim()
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 plan commit lookup failed" }
if ($planCommit -notmatch '^[0-9a-f]{40}$') {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan has no valid commit"
}
& git merge-base --is-ancestor $planCommit HEAD
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
    throw "approved T2.5 plan commit is not in current history"
}

$script:T25PlanningFiles = [ordered]@{
    TaskSplit = "doc/任务拆分.md"
    T23Design = "doc/设计-T2.3-state持久化.md"
    T25Design = "doc/设计-T2.5-filesystem安全操作.md"
    T25Plan   = "doc/计划-T2.5-filesystem安全操作.md"
}
$script:T25PlanningHashes = [ordered]@{}
foreach ($entry in $script:T25PlanningFiles.GetEnumerator()) {
    $resolved = Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $entry.Value -ErrorAction Stop
    $hash = (
        Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $resolved -Algorithm SHA256 -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Hash
    if ($hash -notmatch '^[0-9A-F]{64}$') {
        throw "invalid planning hash for $($entry.Value)"
    }
    $script:T25PlanningHashes[$entry.Key] = $hash
    "T2.5 planning baseline $($entry.Key): $hash"
}

function Assert-T25PlanningBaseline {
    foreach ($entry in $script:T25PlanningFiles.GetEnumerator()) {
        $actual = (
            Get-FileHash `
                -LiteralPath $entry.Value `
                -Algorithm SHA256 `
                -ErrorAction Stop
        ).Hash
        $expected = $script:T25PlanningHashes[$entry.Key]
        if ($actual -ne $expected) {
            throw "T2.5 planning input changed: $($entry.Value)"
        }
    }
}

# TestTask0_DesignHashIsCurrent：立即复算，证明 helper 使用当前工作树内容而非固定旧提交。
Assert-T25PlanningBaseline
```

脚本只能证明计划已跟踪、无未提交变化且提交位于当前历史；开始执行前还必须已有用户对
该提交内容的明确批准。批准前不得把“计划已写完”解释为实现授权。四个 hash 只存在于
当前 `pwsh` 会话；Task 5、Task 6 开始前和 Task 13 收口时都调用
`Assert-T25PlanningBaseline`，任何 planning 输入变化都必须停止并重新审查，不以固定
历史 commit 代替当前文档。

- [ ] **步骤 3：只容忍执行前已经存在的 `.superpowers/`，其他脏项全部阻断**

```powershell
$statusLines = @(
    & git -c core.quotePath=false status `
        --porcelain=v1 `
        --untracked-files=all
)
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 clean-worktree check failed" }

$script:T25SuperpowersBaseline = @(
    $statusLines | Where-Object {
        $_ -match '^\?\? \.superpowers[/\\]'
    }
)
$unexpectedStatus = @(
    $statusLines | Where-Object {
        $_ -notmatch '^\?\? \.superpowers[/\\]'
    }
)
if ($unexpectedStatus.Count -ne 0) {
    $unexpectedStatus
    throw "T2.5 implementation requires a clean worktree outside pre-existing .superpowers"
}
$script:T25StatusBaseline = @($statusLines)

# TestTask0_PreservesConcurrentWorktreeChanges：发现并发/用户改动只停止，不清理现场。
$statusAfterReadOnlyCheck = @(
    & git -c core.quotePath=false status `
        --porcelain=v1 `
        --untracked-files=all
)
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 second status lookup failed" }
if ([string]::Join("`n", $statusAfterReadOnlyCheck) -ne
    [string]::Join("`n", $script:T25StatusBaseline)) {
    throw "read-only Task 0 unexpectedly changed worktree status"
}
```

只有 Task 0 开始前已经存在且为 untracked 的 `.superpowers/` 内容可以保留；本计划
不读取、删除、移动、暂存或改写它。其他 staged/unstaged/untracked/ignored 之外的脏项
全部阻断，包括未跟踪计划和并发修改。`TestTask0_PreservesConcurrentWorktreeChanges`
是脚本级固定名：它证明检查只读且遇到并发变化会停止，不表示允许带着并发变化继续实现。
`$script:T25StatusBaseline` 保存此时完整、有序的 porcelain status；由于上一步已经拒绝
其他条目，它只可能包含当时既有的 `.superpowers/` 行，Task 13 必须逐字节复用。

- [ ] **步骤 4：运行未引入 x/sys 前的完整基线门并动态记录 HEAD**

```powershell
$unformatted = @(& $gofmt -l .)
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline gofmt check failed" }
if ($unformatted.Count -ne 0) {
    $unformatted
    throw "T2.5 baseline contains unformatted Go files"
}
& $go vet ./...
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline go vet failed" }
& $go build -buildvcs=false ./...
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline build failed" }
& $go test ./... -count=1
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline tests failed" }
& git diff --check
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline diff check failed" }

$script:T25BaselineCommit = (& git rev-parse --verify HEAD).Trim()
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 baseline commit lookup failed" }
if ($script:T25BaselineCommit -notmatch '^[0-9a-f]{40}$') {
    throw "T2.5 baseline commit is invalid"
}
Assert-T25PlanningBaseline
"T2.5 implementation baseline: $script:T25BaselineCommit"
```

后续任务在同一 `pwsh` 会话执行；若会话重启，必须从任务 0 重新运行。动态值用于最终
审查实现提交范围；它与四个 planning hash 都不写入版本库，也不得替换为设计文档中的
固定历史提交。任何实现任务开始前发现非 `.superpowers/` 工作树变化，都停止并由用户
裁定；任务自己的已提交变更通过更新 HEAD 推进，不复用旧 shell baseline。

- [ ] **步骤 1：先引入并核验唯一固定依赖**

测试文件从第一行开始就导入 `golang.org/x/sys/windows`，因此必须在编译红灯前建立
模块解析环境；该动作不实现任何 filesystem API：

```powershell
& $go get "golang.org/x/sys@v0.42.0"
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "T2.5 x/sys install failed" }
Assert-XSysPin -Task "T2.5 Task 1 dependency"
```

检查 `go.mod` / `go.sum` 只引入 `v0.42.0` 所需记录。T2.4 若已先引入该版本，本步骤
必须幂等复用；这是整份计划唯一允许执行 `go get golang.org/x/sys...` 的位置。Task 2
以后只调用 `Assert-XSysPin`，不得再次 `go get`、`go mod edit` 或降级依赖。

- [ ] **步骤 2：编写固定名称的失败测试**

先创建 `path_windows_test.go`。以下代码块是该文件的完整首版内容，不是函数名清单；
原样落盘后必须能被 Go 编译器解析并进入目标红灯：

```go
package filesystem

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io/fs"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"unsafe"

	"golang.org/x/sys/windows"

	"github.com/AUTO-MAS-Project/AUTO-MAS-Runtime/internal/protocol"
)

func TestCanonicalize_RejectsUnsafeSyntaxBeforeIO(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	tests := []struct {
		name string
		path string
	}{
		{name: "empty", path: ""},
		{name: "nul", path: "C:\\AUTO-MAS\x00\\repo"},
		{name: "drive relative", path: `C:repo`},
		{name: "dot", path: `.`},
		{name: "parent relative", path: `..\repo`},
		{name: "physical device", path: `\\.\PhysicalDrive0`},
		{name: "global root", path: `\\?\GLOBALROOT\Device`},
		{name: "reserved tail", path: `C:\AUTO-MAS\CON`},
		{name: "alternate stream", path: `C:\AUTO-MAS\name:stream`},
		{name: "empty component", path: `C:\AUTO-MAS\\repo`},
		{name: "trailing dot", path: `C:\AUTO-MAS\repo.`},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			t.Parallel()

			api := newProductionPathAPI()
			api.attributes = func(string) (uint32, error) {
				t.Fatal("attributes called for invalid syntax")
				return 0, nil
			}
			api.openPath = func(string, openSpec) (windows.Handle, error) {
				t.Fatal("openPath called for invalid syntax")
				return windows.InvalidHandle, nil
			}
			got, err := canonicalizeContextWith(t.Context(), test.path, api)
			if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidArgument) {
				t.Fatalf("canonicalizeContextWith(%q) error = %v, want ErrInvalidArgument", test.path, err)
			}
			if got != (CanonicalPath{}) {
				t.Fatalf("canonicalizeContextWith(%q) = %#v, want zero value", test.path, got)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_ContextRejectedBeforeIO(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		ctx  context.Context
		want error
	}{
		{name: "nil", ctx: nil, want: ErrInvalidArgument},
		{
			name: "pre-canceled",
			ctx: func() context.Context {
				ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
				cancel()
				return ctx
			}(),
			want: context.Canceled,
		},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			api := newProductionPathAPI()
			calls := 0
			api.attributes = func(string) (uint32, error) {
				calls++
				return 0, errors.New("unexpected attributes")
			}
			api.openPath = func(string, openSpec) (windows.Handle, error) {
				calls++
				return windows.InvalidHandle, errors.New("unexpected open")
			}
			if _, err := canonicalizeContextWith(
				test.ctx,
				`C:\AUTO-MAS`,
				api,
			); !errors.Is(err, test.want) {
				t.Fatalf("canonicalizeContextWith() error = %v, want %v", err, test.want)
			}
			if calls != 0 {
				t.Fatalf("I/O calls = %d, want 0", calls)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_NormalizesEquivalentForms(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	mixed := filepath.Join(root, "MiXeD")
	if err := os.Mkdir(mixed, 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.Mkdir() error = %v", err)
	}

	canonical := mustCanonicalize(t, mixed)
	forms := []string{
		mixed + `\`,
		filepath.Join(root, "mixed"),
		extendedPathForTest(mixed),
	}
	for _, form := range forms {
		got := mustCanonicalize(t, form)
		if !canonical.Equal(got) {
			t.Errorf("Canonicalize(%q).Equal(canonical) = false, want true", form)
		}
	}
	if !filepath.IsAbs(canonical.String()) {
		t.Fatalf("String() = %q, want absolute path", canonical.String())
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(canonical.Native(), `\\?\`) {
		t.Fatalf("Native() = %q, want extended prefix", canonical.Native())
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_ExpandsExistingShortPath(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	longName := filepath.Join(root, "Directory With A Long Name")
	if err := os.Mkdir(longName, 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.Mkdir() error = %v", err)
	}
	shortName, ok := shortPathForTest(t, longName)
	if !ok {
		t.Skip("volume does not expose an 8.3 short path for the fixture")
	}

	longCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, longName)
	shortCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, shortName)
	if !longCanonical.Equal(shortCanonical) {
		t.Fatalf("long.Equal(short) = false, want true")
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_AllowsOnlySafeNonexistentTail(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	target := filepath.Join(root, "future", "leaf")
	got := mustCanonicalize(t, target)
	parent := mustCanonicalize(t, root)
	if !parent.Contains(got) {
		t.Fatalf("Canonicalize(root).Contains(target) = false, want true")
	}
	if got.String() != target {
		t.Fatalf("String() = %q, want %q", got.String(), target)
	}
}

func TestCanonicalPath_ContainsUsesVolumeAndComponentBoundary(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	parent := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS`)
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		child CanonicalPath
		want  bool
	}{
		{name: "same path", child: canonicalPathForTest(`c:\auto-mas`), want: false},
		{name: "direct child", child: canonicalPathForTest(`c:\auto-mas\repo`), want: true},
		{name: "deep child", child: canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\repo\src`), want: true},
		{name: "sibling prefix", child: canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS-2`), want: false},
		{name: "component prefix", child: canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS-old\repo`), want: false},
		{name: "other volume", child: canonicalPathForTest(`D:\AUTO-MAS\repo`), want: false},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			t.Parallel()
			if got := parent.Contains(test.child); got != test.want {
				t.Fatalf("Contains(%q) = %t, want %t", test.child.String(), got, test.want)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCanonicalPath_UsesOrdinalNonASCIIEquivalence(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	upper := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\Äpfel`)
	lower := canonicalPathForTest(`c:\auto-mas\äPFEL`)
	if !upper.Equal(lower) {
		t.Fatal("ordinal case variants are not equal")
	}
	parent := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\Äpfel`)
	child := canonicalPathForTest(`c:\auto-mas\äPFEL\cache`)
	if !parent.Contains(child) {
		t.Fatal("ordinal case variant child is not contained")
	}

	composed := canonicalPathForTest("C:\\AUTO-MAS\\\u00e9")
	decomposed := canonicalPathForTest("C:\\AUTO-MAS\\e\u0301")
	if composed.Equal(decomposed) {
		t.Fatal("composed and decomposed forms are ordinal-equal, want distinct")
	}
}

func TestCanonicalPath_OrdinalComparisonFailureFailsClosed(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	valid := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\repo`)
	badVolume := valid
	badVolume.volumeKey = "C:\x00"
	if badVolume.Equal(valid) || badVolume.Contains(valid) {
		t.Fatal("invalid volume comparison authorized a path")
	}

	badPath := valid
	badPath.comparisonKey = "C:\\AUTO-MAS\x00\\repo"
	descendant := canonicalPathForTest(`C:\AUTO-MAS\repo\child`)
	if badPath.Equal(valid) || badPath.Contains(descendant) {
		t.Fatal("invalid full-path comparison authorized a path")
	}
}

func TestCanonicalize_ExistingPrefixMatchesAccessMatrix(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	file := filepath.Join(root, "state.json")
	if err := os.WriteFile(file, []byte("{}"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.WriteFile() error = %v", err)
	}

	tests := []struct {
		name      string
		path      string
		directory bool
	}{
		{name: "directory", path: root, directory: true},
		{name: "file", path: file, directory: false},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			api := newProductionPathAPI()
			openPath := api.openPath
			closeHandle := api.closeHandle
			var got openSpec
			closeCalls := 0
			api.openPath = func(path string, spec openSpec) (windows.Handle, error) {
				got = spec
				return openPath(path, spec)
			}
			api.closeHandle = func(handle windows.Handle) error {
				closeCalls++
				return closeHandle(handle)
			}

			if _, err := canonicalizeContextWith(t.Context(), test.path, api); err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("canonicalizeContextWith() error = %v", err)
			}
			want := openSpec{
				access:    windows.FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | windows.SYNCHRONIZE,
				share:     windows.FILE_SHARE_READ | windows.FILE_SHARE_WRITE | windows.FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
				creation:  windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
				options:   windows.FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS | windows.FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
				directory: test.directory,
			}
			if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
				t.Fatalf("openSpec = %#v, want %#v", got, want)
			}
			if closeCalls != 1 {
				t.Fatalf("closeHandle calls = %d, want 1", closeCalls)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestFilesystemEnums_StringAndValid(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	type enum interface {
		String() string
		Valid() bool
	}
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		value enum
		want  string
	}{
		{name: "state backend", value: StateBackend, want: "backend"},
		{name: "state mutation", value: StateMutation, want: "mutation"},
		{name: "state update", value: StateUpdate, want: "update"},
		{name: "state environment", value: StateEnvironment, want: "environment"},
		{name: "state write recover", value: StateWritePhaseRecover, want: "recover"},
		{name: "state write create", value: StateWritePhaseCreate, want: "create"},
		{name: "state write write", value: StateWritePhaseWrite, want: "write"},
		{name: "state write sync", value: StateWritePhaseSync, want: "sync"},
		{name: "state write rename", value: StateWritePhaseRename, want: "rename"},
		{name: "state write finalize", value: StateWritePhaseFinalize, want: "finalize"},
		{name: "state write close", value: StateWritePhaseClose, want: "close"},
		{name: "delete uv cache", value: DeleteUVCache, want: "uv_cache"},
		{name: "delete managed venv", value: DeleteManagedVenv, want: "managed_venv"},
		{name: "delete managed python", value: DeleteManagedPython, want: "managed_python"},
		{name: "delete repo update", value: DeleteRepositoryUpdate, want: "repository_update"},
		{name: "delete repo retired", value: DeleteRepositoryRetired, want: "repository_retired"},
		{name: "delete temporary", value: DeleteDownloadTemporary, want: "download_temporary"},
		{name: "delete uv staging", value: DeleteUVStaging, want: "uv_staging"},
		{name: "delete pycache", value: DeletePythonCache, want: "python_cache"},
		{name: "delete build cache", value: DeleteBuildCache, want: "build_cache"},
		{name: "audit started", value: DeleteAuditStarted, want: "started"},
		{name: "audit finished", value: DeleteAuditFinished, want: "finished"},
		{name: "rename retired", value: RenameRepositoryToRetired, want: "repository_to_retired"},
		{name: "rename repository", value: RenameUpdateToRepository, want: "update_to_repository"},
		{name: "rename uv", value: RenameUVStagingToVersion, want: "uv_staging_to_version"},
	}
	for _, test := range tests {
		t.Run(test.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if !test.value.Valid() {
				t.Fatal("Valid() = false, want true")
			}
			if got := test.value.String(); got != test.want {
				t.Fatalf("String() = %q, want %q", got, test.want)
			}
		})
	}

	invalid := []enum{
		StateFileKind(""),
		StateFileKind("unknown"),
		StateWritePhase(""),
		StateWritePhase("unknown"),
		DeleteKind(""),
		DeleteKind("unknown"),
		DeleteAuditPhase(""),
		DeleteAuditPhase("unknown"),
		RenameKind(""),
		RenameKind("unknown"),
	}
	for _, value := range invalid {
		if value.Valid() {
			t.Errorf("%T(%q).Valid() = true, want false", value, value.String())
		}
	}
}

func TestFilesystemErrors_PreserveChainsAndCodes(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	cause := errors.New("injected")
	pathErr := &Error{
		code:      protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot,
		Operation: "authorize",
		Path:      `C:\AUTO-MAS\repo`,
		Err:       cause,
	}
	if !errors.Is(pathErr, cause) {
		t.Fatalf("errors.Is(%v, cause) = false, want true", pathErr)
	}
	if got := pathErr.Code(); got != protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot {
		t.Fatalf("Code() = %q, want %q", got, protocol.CodePathOutsideManagedRoot)
	}

	fileErr := &FileError{Operation: "open", Path: pathErr.Path, Err: cause}
	if !errors.Is(fileErr, cause) {
		t.Fatalf("errors.Is(%v, cause) = false, want true", fileErr)
	}
	tooLarge := &FileError{
		Operation: "size-limit",
		Path:      pathErr.Path,
		Err:       ErrStateFileTooLarge,
	}
	if !errors.Is(tooLarge, ErrStateFileTooLarge) {
		t.Fatalf("errors.Is(%v, ErrStateFileTooLarge) = false, want true", tooLarge)
	}
	auditErr := &AuditError{
		Phase:           DeleteAuditFinished,
		MutationApplied: true,
		Cause:           cause,
	}
	if !errors.Is(auditErr, cause) {
		t.Fatalf("errors.Is(%v, cause) = false, want true", auditErr)
	}

	cleanup := errors.New("cleanup failed")
	writeErr := &StateWriteError{
		Phase:            StateWritePhaseFinalize,
		MutationApplied:  true,
		RecoveryRequired: true,
		Cause:            fileErr,
		CleanupError:     cleanup,
	}
	if !errors.Is(writeErr, cause) || !errors.Is(writeErr, cleanup) {
		t.Fatalf("StateWriteError causes = %v, want primary and cleanup", writeErr)
	}
	if writeErr.Phase != StateWritePhaseFinalize ||
		!writeErr.MutationApplied ||
		!writeErr.RecoveryRequired {
		t.Fatalf("StateWriteError = %#v, want finalize/applied/recovery", writeErr)
	}

	removeErr := &StateRemoveError{Cause: ErrIdentityChanged, CleanupError: cleanup}
	if !errors.Is(removeErr, ErrIdentityChanged) || !errors.Is(removeErr, cleanup) {
		t.Fatalf("StateRemoveError causes = %v, want identity and cleanup", removeErr)
	}
	var typedRemove *StateRemoveError
	if !errors.As(removeErr, &typedRemove) ||
		typedRemove.Cause != ErrIdentityChanged ||
		typedRemove.CleanupError != cleanup {
		t.Fatalf("StateRemoveError = %#v, want public cause fields", typedRemove)
	}
	if got := removeErr.Error(); got == "" ||
		strings.Contains(got, cleanup.Error()) {
		t.Fatalf("StateRemoveError.Error() = %q, want stable text without cause payload", got)
	}
	if errors.Is(fs.ErrNotExist, ErrStateFileNotFound) {
		t.Fatal("raw fs.ErrNotExist matches ErrStateFileNotFound")
	}
	for _, sentinel := range []error{
		ErrStateFileNotFound,
		ErrStateRecoveryRequired,
	} {
		if sentinel == nil || sentinel.Error() == "" {
			t.Fatalf("sentinel = %v, want stable non-empty error", sentinel)
		}
	}
	if got := ErrPOSIXUnlinkUnsupported.Error(); got !=
		"filesystem POSIX unlink is unsupported" {
		t.Fatalf("ErrPOSIXUnlinkUnsupported.Error() = %q", got)
	}
}

func TestWindows_CanonicalPathLongAndShortFormsCannotBypassProtection(t *testing.T) {
	root := t.TempDir()
	longPath := root
	for i := 0; i < 12; i++ {
		longPath = filepath.Join(longPath, strings.Repeat("segment", 4))
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(extendedPathForTest(longPath), 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("os.MkdirAll() error = %v", err)
	}
	longCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, longPath)
	extendedCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, extendedPathForTest(longPath))
	if !longCanonical.Equal(extendedCanonical) {
		t.Fatal("long and extended forms are not equal")
	}

	sibling := mustCanonicalize(t, longPath+"-other")
	if longCanonical.Contains(sibling) {
		t.Fatal("sibling prefix was accepted as a descendant")
	}
	if shortPath, ok := shortPathForTest(t, longPath); ok {
		shortCanonical := mustCanonicalize(t, shortPath)
		if !longCanonical.Equal(shortCanonical) {
			t.Fatal("long and 8.3 forms are not equal")
		}
	}
}

func mustCanonicalize(t *testing.T, path string) CanonicalPath {
	t.Helper()
	got, err := Canonicalize(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Canonicalize(%q) error = %v", path, err)
	}
	return got
}

func canonicalPathForTest(path string) CanonicalPath {
	clean := filepath.Clean(path)
	volume := filepath.VolumeName(clean)
	return CanonicalPath{
		display:       clean,
		native:        extendedPathForTest(clean),
		comparisonKey: clean,
		volumeKey:     volume,
	}
}

func extendedPathForTest(path string) string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\?\`) {
		return path
	}
	if strings.HasPrefix(path, `\\`) {
		return `\\?\UNC\` + strings.TrimPrefix(path, `\\`)
	}
	return `\\?\` + path
}

func shortPathForTest(t *testing.T, path string) (string, bool) {
	t.Helper()

	pathUTF16, err := windows.UTF16PtrFromString(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("windows.UTF16PtrFromString() error = %v", err)
	}
	buffer := make([]uint16, 32768)
	procedure := windows.NewLazySystemDLL("kernel32.dll").NewProc("GetShortPathNameW")
	length, _, callErr := procedure.Call(
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(pathUTF16)),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&buffer[0])),
		uintptr(len(buffer)),
	)
	if length == 0 {
		if errors.Is(callErr, windows.ERROR_SUCCESS) {
			t.Fatalf("GetShortPathNameW(%q) returned no path", path)
		}
		return "", false
	}
	if length >= uintptr(len(buffer)) {
		t.Fatalf("GetShortPathNameW length = %d, buffer = %d", length, len(buffer))
	}
	short := windows.UTF16ToString(buffer[:length])
	if short == path || !strings.Contains(short, "~") {
		return "", false
	}
	return short, true
}
```

测试 helper 只用精确字符串相等和 `~` 标记判断 volume 是否实际提供了不同的 8.3
别名，不把大小写比较用于授权。测试对身份的断言全部通过 `Equal` / `Contains`。

`TestCanonicalize_RejectsUnsafeSyntaxBeforeIO` 表驱动传空字符串、NUL、`C:repo`、
`.`、`..\repo`、`\\.\PhysicalDrive0`、`\\?\GLOBALROOT\Device`、不存在尾部中的
`CON`、`name:stream`、空组件和尾部点，断言 `errors.Is(err,
ErrInvalidArgument)`。测试注入一个一旦调用就 `t.Fatal` 的 path probe，证明纯语法错误
在任何 Win32 查询前拒绝。

等价路径表只断言公开 `Equal` / `Contains` 结果，固定覆盖普通绝对路径、尾部反斜杠、
大小写变体、`\\?\` 形式、可用时的 8.3 短路径和超过 `MAX_PATH` 的普通组件链；
严禁断言 `comparisonKey` / `volumeKey` 的原始字节相等。`Native()` 必须以 `\\?\`
或 `\\?\UNC\` 开头，`String()` 必须是绝对显示路径。`Contains` 表明确断言根本身为
false、直接后代和多级后代为 true、`AUTO-MAS-2` 与组件 `repo-old` 为 false、其他
volume 为 false。

非 ASCII 表固定断言 `Äpfel` / `äPFEL` 在同一组件位置 ordinal ignore-case 等价，
预组合 `é` 与分解序列 `e\u0301` 不等价；测试不得调用 `strings.ToLower`、
`strings.EqualFold`、Unicode normalization 或任何 locale-sensitive 比较。
`TestCanonicalPath_OrdinalComparisonFailureFailsClosed` 用同包私有字段构造含 NUL 的
volume 与完整路径各一例，使 `windows.UTF16FromString` 返回错误，逐项断言 `Equal` /
`Contains` 都为 false 且不 panic；这证明任一 ordinal 调用失败都会关闭授权。已存在
前缀测试捕获 `openSpec`，逐字段断言
Canonicalize 行的 access/share/creation/options；目录前缀断言 `directory=true`，
最终已存在普通文件断言 `directory=false`，并证明规范化结束即关闭该只读 handle。

枚举测试必须逐值列出四个 `StateFileKind`、七个 `StateWritePhase`、九个
`DeleteKind`、两个 `DeleteAuditPhase` 和三个 `RenameKind`，对合法值断言
`String()` 等于冻结字面量且 `Valid()` 为 true，对零值和 `"unknown"` 断言 false。

Windows 对抗测试在真实 `t.TempDir()` 下建立超过 `MAX_PATH` 的组件链；若 volume
启用 8.3，则用 `GetShortPathNameW` 取得同一目录短名。长名、短名、大小写变体和
`\\?\` 形式必须 Equal，且任何形式都不能把 sibling 前缀误判为受保护目录后代。

- [ ] **步骤 4：建立公共类型**

本任务只把“冻结公共契约”中的公共值类型、请求、结果、接口、枚举和 sentinel
分别放入对应主题文件；除 `Canonicalize`、值对象 getter、枚举 `String` / `Valid`
和错误方法外，不提前定义公共方法。构造器与 I/O 方法在各自对应的 `_windows.go`
任务中一次定义，避免先提交会被误用的半实现。**本任务不定义 `Operator`、
`Option`、私有 `options`、`New`、`WithWait` 或 `WithRenameDelays`**；这些声明与实现
在 Task 9 同一提交引入。公共错误实现固定使用：

```go
func (e *Error) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s %q: %v", e.Operation, e.Path, e.Err)
}

func (e *Error) Unwrap() error       { return e.Err }
func (e *Error) Code() protocol.Code { return e.code }

func (e *FileError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s %q: %v", e.Operation, e.Path, e.Err)
}

func (e *FileError) Unwrap() error { return e.Err }

type operationCleanupError struct {
	cause   error
	cleanup error
}

func (e *operationCleanupError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%v; cleanup: %v", e.cause, e.cleanup)
}

func (e *operationCleanupError) Unwrap() []error {
	if e == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return []error{e.cause, e.cleanup}
}

func joinOperationCleanup(cause, cleanup error) error {
	if cause == nil {
		return cleanup
	}
	if cleanup == nil {
		return cause
	}
	if combined, ok := cause.(*operationCleanupError); ok {
		return &operationCleanupError{
			cause:   combined.cause,
			cleanup: errors.Join(combined.cleanup, cleanup),
		}
	}
	return &operationCleanupError{cause: cause, cleanup: cleanup}
}

func splitOperationCleanup(err error) (error, error) {
	if combined, ok := err.(*operationCleanupError); ok {
		return combined.cause, combined.cleanup
	}
	return err, nil
}

func (e *AuditError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("record %s deletion audit: %v", e.Phase, e.Cause)
}

func (e *AuditError) Unwrap() error { return e.Cause }

func (e *StateWriteError) Error() string {
	if e == nil {
		return "state write failed"
	}
	if e.Cause == nil {
		return fmt.Sprintf("state write %s failed", e.Phase)
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("state write %s: %v", e.Phase, e.Cause)
}

func (e *StateWriteError) Unwrap() []error {
	if e == nil {
		return nil
	}
	causes := make([]error, 0, 2)
	if e.Cause != nil {
		causes = append(causes, e.Cause)
	}
	if e.CleanupError != nil {
		causes = append(causes, e.CleanupError)
	}
	return causes
}

func (e *StateRemoveError) Error() string {
	if e == nil {
		return "state remove failed"
	}
	switch {
	case e.Cause != nil && e.CleanupError != nil:
		return "state remove failed; cleanup also failed"
	case e.Cause != nil:
		return "state remove failed"
	case e.CleanupError != nil:
		return "state remove cleanup failed"
	default:
		return "state remove failed"
	}
}

func (e *StateRemoveError) Unwrap() []error {
	if e == nil {
		return nil
	}
	causes := make([]error, 0, 2)
	if e.Cause != nil {
		causes = append(causes, e.Cause)
	}
	if e.CleanupError != nil {
		causes = append(causes, e.CleanupError)
	}
	return causes
}
```

所有 `String` 对未知值返回原始字符串；`Valid` 使用 switch，不保存可变 map。红灯输出
必须匹配步骤 3 的目标 API 缺失；若出现 `no required module provides package`、
`missing go.sum entry` 或网络/代理错误，说明步骤 1 未完成，不能把该结果算作 TDD 红灯。
